- **iac.analyze**: Perform a non-destructive cross-artifact consistency and quality analysis across spec.md, plan.md, and tasks.md after task generation. - **iac.checklist**: Generate a custom checklist for the current infrastructure component based on user requirements. ([kbrockhoff/terraform-aws-lakehouse](https://github.com/kbrockhoff/terraform-aws-lakehouse/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/iac.checklist.md) · 2 copies)
- **iac.clarify**: Identify underspecified areas in the current infrastructure spec by asking up to 5 highly targeted clarification questions and encoding answers back into the spec. ([kbrockhoff/terraform-aws-lakehouse](https://github.com/kbrockhoff/terraform-aws-lakehouse/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/iac.clarify.md) · 2 copies)
- **iac.converge**: Assess the current Terraform codebase against the infrastructure spec, plan, and tasks, then append any remaining unbuilt work as new tasks to tasks.md so implement can complete it. - **ie-techniques**: 1. **Research-backed only** - Every technique must cite peer-reviewed research or documented empirical results 2. **Composable patterns** - Techniques should combine with each other; never use one in isolation 3. **Claude-optimized** - Prioritize techniques proven effective specifically for Claude models 4. **Measurable impact** - Each technique should have observable, verifiable effects on output ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/ie-techniques.md) · ★13)
- **ie-techniques**: Reference for the 16 proven instruction engineering techniques. Invoke via /ie-techniques when crafting prompts. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/ie-techniques.md) · ★13)
- **ie-template**: Template and example for engineered instructions. Invoke via /ie-template when drafting prompts. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/ie-template.md) · ★13)
- **ie-template**: 1. **Template is a starting point** — Adapt sections to task requirements; not every section is mandatory 2. **Examples over descriptions** — One concrete example teaches more than paragraphs of explanation 3. **Emotional framing is mandatory** — Every instruction must include at least one emotional stimulus 4. **Self-check closes the loop** — Verification steps prevent unverifiable outputs ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/ie-template.md) · ★13)
- **ie-tool-docs**: Guidance for writing tool/function documentation. Invoke via /ie-tool-docs when defining MCP tools, APIs, or CLI commands. ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/commands/ie-tool-docs.md) · ★13)
- **ie-tool-docs**: 1. **Equal effort to prompts** - Tool definitions deserve as much attention as prompt engineering 2. **Document the unhappy path** - Error cases and edge conditions matter more than the happy path 3. **Show, don't tell** - Every parameter needs a concrete example value 4. **Prevent misuse explicitly** - "When NOT to use" is as important as "when to use" ([axiomantic/spellbook](https://github.com/axiomantic/spellbook/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/ie-tool-docs.md) · ★13)

- **if**: The **\&lt;\&lt;if\&gt;\&gt;** command evaluates its condition, and based on that decides which statements to execute next. This is equivalent to `if` keyword in most programming languages. This command may have multiple parts, which look as follows: ([flame-engine/flame](https://github.com/flame-engine/flame/blob/HEAD/doc/other_modules/jenny/language/commands/if.md) · ★11k · 2 copies)
- **if**: Runs a line or a block only when a condition is true. ([ilpersi/EasyUOReforged](https://github.com/ilpersi/EasyUOReforged/blob/HEAD/website/src/content/docs/commands/if.md) · ★8)
- **if-compare**: Compare treatment of a product across multiple jurisdictions. ([panaversity/agentfactory-business-plugins](https://github.com/panaversity/agentfactory-business-plugins/blob/HEAD/islamic-finance/commands/if-compare.md) · ★38)
- **if-journal**: Generate jurisdiction-aware journal entries or amortisation/distribution schedules for an Islamic finance transaction. ([panaversity/agentfactory-business-plugins](https://github.com/panaversity/agentfactory-business-plugins/blob/HEAD/islamic-finance/commands/if-journal.md) · ★38)
- **if-screen**: Run Shariah compliance screening on a company or portfolio. ([panaversity/agentfactory-business-plugins](https://github.com/panaversity/agentfactory-business-plugins/blob/HEAD/islamic-finance/commands/if-screen.md) · ★38)
- **if-zakat**: Compute zakat for an entity under a specific jurisdiction's rules. ([panaversity/agentfactory-business-plugins](https://github.com/panaversity/agentfactory-business-plugins/blob/HEAD/islamic-finance/commands/if-zakat.md) · ★38)
- **iFacto.chatexport**: Export a Claude Code chat session to markdown (diagnose + conversation + full) as analysis material for improving the iFacto dev workflow. ([iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook](https://github.com/iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook/blob/HEAD/claudefiles/commands/iFacto.chatexport.md))
- **iFacto.CodeReview**: Review your AL changes (working tree / branch) or an Azure DevOps PR the way an iFacto devlead does: rulebook findings with rule ids, requirement check against Jira, BCQuality and security pass, one-word recommendation. Read-only: never posts, never votes. Runs on Claude Opus: the agent carries `model: opus`, and when the review is run inside this session instead, switch with `/model opus` first. ([iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook](https://github.com/iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook/blob/HEAD/claudefiles/commands/iFacto.CodeReview.md))
- **iFacto.Pullrequest**: Apply all steps to create a pull request for your new developments — includes JIRA validation, a mandatory test run on SaaS sandboxes, a mandatory iFacto code review, documentation, and Azure DevOps PR creation. ([iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook](https://github.com/iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook/blob/HEAD/claudefiles/commands/iFacto.Pullrequest.md))
- **iFacto.SDD**: Start the iFacto SDD agent (Red-Green-Mutate) in THIS session for the request you type after the command. Use for SDD, TDD, test-driven work, 'gebruik de SDD', writing tests first, or implementing a feature with tests. Never delegate SDD to a subagent — run this command instead. ([iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook](https://github.com/iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook/blob/HEAD/claudefiles/commands/iFacto.SDD.md))
- **iFacto.SolutionDesigner**: Start the iFacto Solution Designer in THIS session for the JIRA issue or feature you type after the command: it analyses the requirement, grounds the design in the iFacto company rules (BCQuality) and DistriApps constraints, and writes the instruction document in App/Docs/Instructions/. Runs on the session's model, so switch to Claude Opus first (`/model opus`): the design drives every later step and is not Sonnet work. Use for 'design this', 'maak een design', 'schrijf een instruction document', or any request to design before coding. ([iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook](https://github.com/iFacto-Business-Solutions-NV/ifacto-claude-playbook/blob/HEAD/claudefiles/commands/iFacto.SolutionDesigner.md))
